#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The benchmark uses a fixed random graph and records the intermediate vertex on each strict improvement. I’m using a two-dimensional MPI process grid with batched pivot exchanges to reduce communication while preserving the original update order for both distances and paths.

+struct Partition {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+Partition partition(size_t n, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    return {coordinate * base + std::min(extra, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate)),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < extra)};
+}
+
+int owner(size_t k, size_t n, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (base + 1) * extra;
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (base + 1)
+                                         : extra + (k - boundary) / base);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm comm;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm;
+    MPI_Comm colComm;
+    int rank;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2];
+    Partition rows;
+    Partition cols;
+
+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dims);
+        const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rows = partition(n, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        cols = partition(n, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    }
+
+    void free() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};
+
+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per result. Exponentiate
+// its affine transformation to jump directly to any position in the ORIGINAL
+// random stream; changing the seed per rank would change the benchmark graph.
+struct RandomJump {
+    unsigned int multiplier = 1;
+    unsigned int increment = 0;
+
+    explicit RandomJump(size_t samples) {
+        unsigned int a = 1;
+        unsigned int c = 0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
+            a *= 1103515245u;
+            c = c * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        }
+        while (samples != 0) {
+            if (samples & 1) {
+                increment = increment * a + c;
+                multiplier *= a;
+            }
+            c *= a + 1;
+            a *= a;
+            samples >>= 1;
+        }
+    }
+
+    unsigned int operator()(unsigned int seed) const {
+        return multiplier * seed + increment;
+    }
+};
+#endif
+
+void initializeMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path, size_t n,
+                        const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+    if (rows != 0 && cols != 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = RandomJump(grid.rows.begin * n + grid.cols.begin)(42);
+        const RandomJump nextRow(n - cols);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+                dist[i * cols + j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            seed = nextRow(seed);
+        }
+    }
+#else
+    // Other libc implementations need not use the same RNG recurrence.
+    // Stream one original row at a time without allocating a global matrix.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        std::vector<unsigned int> row(n);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                row[j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            for (int c = 0; c < grid.dims[1]; ++c) {
+                const Partition columns = partition(n, grid.dims[1], c);
+                if (columns.size == 0) continue;
+                int coordinates[2] = {owner(i, n, grid.dims[0]), c};
+                int target;
+                MPI_Cart_rank(grid.comm, coordinates, &target);
+                if (target == 0) {
+                    std::copy_n(row.data() + columns.begin, columns.size,
+                                dist.data() + (i - grid.rows.begin) * cols);
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(row.data() + columns.begin, static_cast<int>(columns.size),
+                             MPI_UNSIGNED, target, 0, grid.comm);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (cols != 0) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data() + i * cols, static_cast<int>(cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                     0, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+    }
+#endif

+            path[i * cols + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.cols.begin + j);

+// Independent, contiguous updates permit SIMD without changing strict tie

+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivot,
+                      unsigned int via, size_t count, unsigned int k) {

+        const unsigned int candidate = via + pivot[j];
+        const unsigned int previous = dist[j];
+        dist[j] = std::min(previous, candidate);
+        path[j] = candidate < previous ? k : path[j];

+void updateRectangle(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& rowPanel,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& colPanel,
+                     size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t width, size_t firstPivot,
+                     size_t rowBegin, size_t rowEnd, size_t colBegin, size_t colEnd) {
+    // Keep distance/path tiles in cache while replaying a whole pivot batch.
+    constexpr size_t tileRows = 32;
+    constexpr size_t tileCols = 256;
+    for (size_t ib = rowBegin; ib < rowEnd; ib += tileRows) {
+        const size_t ie = std::min(ib + tileRows, rowEnd);
+        for (size_t jb = colBegin; jb < colEnd; jb += tileCols) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(tileCols, colEnd - jb);
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                const unsigned int* pivot = rowPanel.data() + t * cols + jb;
+                for (size_t i = ib; i < ie; ++i) {
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + i * cols + jb,
+                              path.data() + i * cols + jb, pivot,
+                              colPanel[t * rows + i], count,
+                              static_cast<unsigned int>(firstPivot + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
     }

-void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
-    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
-            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    // Bound MPI counts even when a panel would otherwise exceed INT_MAX.
+    const size_t maxEdge = std::max<size_t>(1, (n + grid.dims[1] - 1) / grid.dims[1]);
+    const size_t batch = std::min<size_t>(64, INT_MAX / maxEdge);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> diagonalRows(batch * batch);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> diagonalCols(batch * batch);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowPanel(batch * cols);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> colPanel(batch * rows);
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n;) {
+        const int rowOwner = owner(first, n, grid.dims[0]);
+        const int colOwner = owner(first, n, grid.dims[1]);
+        const Partition pivotRows = partition(n, grid.dims[0], rowOwner);
+        const Partition pivotCols = partition(n, grid.dims[1], colOwner);
+        const size_t width = std::min({batch, pivotRows.begin + pivotRows.size - first,
+                                      pivotCols.begin + pivotCols.size - first});
+        const bool ownsRows = grid.coords[0] == rowOwner;
+        const bool ownsCols = grid.coords[1] == colOwner;
+        const size_t ri = ownsRows ? first - grid.rows.begin : 0;
+        const size_t cj = ownsCols ? first - grid.cols.begin : 0;
+
+        // 1. Solve the diagonal tile, retaining its row/column at EACH k.
+        // Using only its final values would change strict-improvement paths.
+        if (ownsRows && ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols + cj, width,
+                            diagonalRows.data() + t * width);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    diagonalCols[t * width + i] = dist[(ri + i) * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols + cj,
+                              path.data() + (ri + i) * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width,
+                              diagonalCols[t * width + i], width,
+                              static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        int requestCount = 0;
+        if (ownsRows) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalCols.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalRows.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 2. Solve the pivot row/column panels, saving the chronological
+        // pivot values needed by all the independent remaining rectangles.
+        if (ownsRows && cols != 0) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols, cols,
+                            rowPanel.data() + t * cols);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    unsigned int* d = dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    unsigned int* p = path.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    const unsigned int via = diagonalCols[t * width + i];
+                    if (ownsCols) {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cj,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                        const size_t after = cj + width;
+                        updateRow(d + after, p + after, rowPanel.data() + t * cols + after,
+                                  via, cols - after, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    } else {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cols,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    colPanel[t * rows + i] = dist[i * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    if (ownsRows && i >= ri && i < ri + width) continue;
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + i * cols + cj, path.data() + i * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width, colPanel[t * rows + i],
+                              width, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(rowPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(colPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * rows), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 3. Replay exactly the original increasing-k updates locally.
+        const size_t rowCuts[4] = {0, ownsRows ? ri : rows,
+                                  ownsRows ? ri + width : rows, rows};
+        const size_t colCuts[4] = {0, ownsCols ? cj : cols,
+                                  ownsCols ? cj + width : cols, cols};
+        for (int r = 0; r < 4; r += 2) {
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; c += 2) {
+                updateRectangle(dist, path, rowPanel, colPanel, rows, cols, width, first,
+                                rowCuts[r], rowCuts[r + 1], colCuts[c], colCuts[c + 1]);
+            }
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
+        first += width;
     }
 }

+// Only materialize the global matrix when the requested output needs it.
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                                      size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(n * n);
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(grid.comm, &processes);
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < processes; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const Partition rows = partition(n, grid.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const Partition cols = partition(n, grid.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows.size == 0 || cols.size == 0) continue;
+            unsigned int* destination = result.data() + rows.begin * n + cols.begin;
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(local.data() + i * cols.size, cols.size, destination + i * n);
                 }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype block;
+                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows.size), static_cast<int>(cols.size),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), MPI_UNSIGNED, &block);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+                MPI_Recv(destination, 1, block, rank, 1, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&block);
             }
         }
+    } else if (!local.empty()) {
+        MPI_Datatype row;
+        MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(grid.cols.size), MPI_UNSIGNED, &row);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&row);
+        MPI_Send(local.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.size), row, 0, 1, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&row);
     }
+    return result;
 }

+struct Partition {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+Partition partition(size_t n, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    return {coordinate * base + std::min(extra, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate)),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < extra)};
+}
+
+int owner(size_t k, size_t n, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (base + 1) * extra;
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (base + 1)
+                                         : extra + (k - boundary) / base);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm comm;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm;
+    MPI_Comm colComm;
+    int rank;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2];
+    Partition rows;
+    Partition cols;
+
+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dims);
+        const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rows = partition(n, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        cols = partition(n, dims[1], coords[1]);
+    }
+
+    void free() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+};
+
+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per result. Exponentiate
+// its affine transformation to jump directly to any position in the ORIGINAL
+// random stream; changing the seed per rank would change the benchmark graph.
+struct RandomJump {
+    unsigned int multiplier = 1;
+    unsigned int increment = 0;
+
+    explicit RandomJump(size_t samples) {
+        unsigned int a = 1;
+        unsigned int c = 0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
+            a *= 1103515245u;
+            c = c * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        }
+        while (samples != 0) {
+            if (samples & 1) {
+                increment = increment * a + c;
+                multiplier *= a;
+            }
+            c *= a + 1;
+            a *= a;
+            samples >>= 1;
+        }
+    }
+
+    unsigned int operator()(unsigned int seed) const {
+        return multiplier * seed + increment;
+    }
+};
+#endif
+
+void initializeMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path, size_t n,
+                        const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+    if (rows != 0 && cols != 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = RandomJump(grid.rows.begin * n + grid.cols.begin)(42);
+        const RandomJump nextRow(n - cols);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+                dist[i * cols + j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            seed = nextRow(seed);
+        }
+    }
+#else

+    // Stream one original row at a time without allocating a global matrix.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {

+                row[j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            for (int c = 0; c < grid.dims[1]; ++c) {
+                const Partition columns = partition(n, grid.dims[1], c);
+                if (columns.size == 0) continue;
+                int coordinates[2] = {owner(i, n, grid.dims[0]), c};

+            }
+        }

+            MPI_Recv(dist.data() + i * cols, static_cast<int>(cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                     0, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+    }
+#endif

+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            path[i * cols + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.cols.begin + j);
+            if (grid.rows.begin + i == grid.cols.begin + j) dist[i * cols + j] = 0;
+        }
+    }

+                              diagonalCols[t * width + i], width,
+                              static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        int requestCount = 0;
+        if (ownsRows) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalCols.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalRows.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 2. Solve the pivot row/column panels, saving the chronological
+        // pivot values needed by all the independent remaining rectangles.
+        if (ownsRows && cols != 0) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols, cols,
+                            rowPanel.data() + t * cols);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    unsigned int* d = dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    unsigned int* p = path.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    const unsigned int via = diagonalCols[t * width + i];
+                    if (ownsCols) {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cj,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                        const size_t after = cj + width;
+                        updateRow(d + after, p + after, rowPanel.data() + t * cols + after,
+                                  via, cols - after, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    } else {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cols,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    colPanel[t * rows + i] = dist[i * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    if (ownsRows && i >= ri && i < ri + width) continue;
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + i * cols + cj, path.data() + i * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width, colPanel[t * rows + i],
+                              width, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(rowPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(colPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * rows), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 3. Replay exactly the original increasing-k updates locally.
+        const size_t rowCuts[4] = {0, ownsRows ? ri : rows,
+                                  ownsRows ? ri + width : rows, rows};
+        const size_t colCuts[4] = {0, ownsCols ? cj : cols,
+                                  ownsCols ? cj + width : cols, cols};
+        for (int r = 0; r < 4; r += 2) {
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; c += 2) {
+                updateRectangle(dist, path, rowPanel, colPanel, rows, cols, width, first,
+                                rowCuts[r], rowCuts[r + 1], colCuts[c], colCuts[c + 1]);
+            }
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
+        first += width;
     }
 }

+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;

+        result.resize(n * n);
+        int processes;

                 }

+                MPI_Datatype block;
+                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows.size), static_cast<int>(cols.size),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), MPI_UNSIGNED, &block);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+                MPI_Recv(destination, 1, block, rank, 1, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&block);
             }
         }
+    } else if (!local.empty()) {
+        MPI_Datatype row;
+        MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(grid.cols.size), MPI_UNSIGNED, &row);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&row);
+        MPI_Send(local.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.size), row, 0, 1, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&row);
     }
+    return result;

+struct Partition {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+Partition partition(size_t n, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    return {coordinate * base + std::min(extra, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate)),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < extra)};
+}

+int owner(size_t k, size_t n, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (base + 1) * extra;
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (base + 1)
+                                         : extra + (k - boundary) / base);

+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm comm;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm;
+    MPI_Comm colComm;
+    int rank;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};

+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dims);

+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);

+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per result. Exponentiate
+// its affine transformation to jump directly to any position in the ORIGINAL
+// random stream; changing the seed per rank would change the benchmark graph.
+struct RandomJump {
+    unsigned int multiplier = 1;
+    unsigned int increment = 0;
+
+    explicit RandomJump(size_t samples) {
+        unsigned int a = 1;
+        unsigned int c = 0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
+            a *= 1103515245u;
+            c = c * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        }
+        while (samples != 0) {
+            if (samples & 1) {
+                increment = increment * a + c;
+                multiplier *= a;
+            }
+            c *= a + 1;
+            a *= a;
+            samples >>= 1;
+        }
     }
+
+    unsigned int operator()(unsigned int seed) const {
+        return multiplier * seed + increment;
+    }
+};
+#endif

+void initializeMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path, size_t n,
+                        const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+    if (rows != 0 && cols != 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = RandomJump(grid.rows.begin * n + grid.cols.begin)(42);
+        const RandomJump nextRow(n - cols);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+                dist[i * cols + j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            seed = nextRow(seed);
+        }
+    }
+#else
+    // Other libc implementations need not use the same RNG recurrence.
+    // Stream one original row at a time without allocating a global matrix.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        std::vector<unsigned int> row(n);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                row[j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            for (int c = 0; c < grid.dims[1]; ++c) {
+                const Partition columns = partition(n, grid.dims[1], c);
+                if (columns.size == 0) continue;

+                int target;

+                if (target == 0) {
+                    std::copy_n(row.data() + columns.begin, columns.size,
+                                dist.data() + (i - grid.rows.begin) * cols);
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(row.data() + columns.begin, static_cast<int>(columns.size),
+                             MPI_UNSIGNED, target, 0, grid.comm);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (cols != 0) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data() + i * cols, static_cast<int>(cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                     0, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            path[i * cols + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.cols.begin + j);
+            if (grid.rows.begin + i == grid.cols.begin + j) dist[i * cols + j] = 0;
+        }
+    }

+// Independent, contiguous updates permit SIMD without changing strict tie
+// handling or the original meaning of path[i,j] (last improving pivot).
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivot,
+                      unsigned int via, size_t count, unsigned int k) {
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = via + pivot[j];
+        const unsigned int previous = dist[j];
+        dist[j] = std::min(previous, candidate);
+        path[j] = candidate < previous ? k : path[j];
+    }

+void updateRectangle(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& rowPanel,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& colPanel,
+                     size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t width, size_t firstPivot,
+                     size_t rowBegin, size_t rowEnd, size_t colBegin, size_t colEnd) {
+    // Keep distance/path tiles in cache while replaying a whole pivot batch.
+    constexpr size_t tileRows = 32;

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    // Bound MPI counts even when a panel would otherwise exceed INT_MAX.
+    const size_t maxEdge = std::max<size_t>(1, (n + grid.dims[1] - 1) / grid.dims[1]);
+    const size_t batch = std::min<size_t>(64, INT_MAX / maxEdge);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> diagonalRows(batch * batch);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> diagonalCols(batch * batch);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowPanel(batch * cols);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> colPanel(batch * rows);
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n;) {
+        const int rowOwner = owner(first, n, grid.dims[0]);
+        const int colOwner = owner(first, n, grid.dims[1]);
+        const Partition pivotRows = partition(n, grid.dims[0], rowOwner);
+        const Partition pivotCols = partition(n, grid.dims[1], colOwner);
+        const size_t width = std::min({batch, pivotRows.begin + pivotRows.size - first,
+                                      pivotCols.begin + pivotCols.size - first});
+        const bool ownsRows = grid.coords[0] == rowOwner;
+        const bool ownsCols = grid.coords[1] == colOwner;
+        const size_t ri = ownsRows ? first - grid.rows.begin : 0;
+        const size_t cj = ownsCols ? first - grid.cols.begin : 0;
+
+        // 1. Solve the diagonal tile, retaining its row/column at EACH k.
+        // Using only its final values would change strict-improvement paths.
+        if (ownsRows && ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols + cj, width,
+                            diagonalRows.data() + t * width);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    diagonalCols[t * width + i] = dist[(ri + i) * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols + cj,
+                              path.data() + (ri + i) * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width,
+                              diagonalCols[t * width + i], width,
+                              static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        int requestCount = 0;
+        if (ownsRows) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalCols.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalRows.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 2. Solve the pivot row/column panels, saving the chronological
+        // pivot values needed by all the independent remaining rectangles.
+        if (ownsRows && cols != 0) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols, cols,
+                            rowPanel.data() + t * cols);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    unsigned int* d = dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    unsigned int* p = path.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    const unsigned int via = diagonalCols[t * width + i];
+                    if (ownsCols) {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cj,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                        const size_t after = cj + width;
+                        updateRow(d + after, p + after, rowPanel.data() + t * cols + after,
+                                  via, cols - after, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    } else {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cols,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    colPanel[t * rows + i] = dist[i * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    if (ownsRows && i >= ri && i < ri + width) continue;
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + i * cols + cj, path.data() + i * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width, colPanel[t * rows + i],
+                              width, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(rowPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(colPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * rows), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 3. Replay exactly the original increasing-k updates locally.
+        const size_t rowCuts[4] = {0, ownsRows ? ri : rows,
+                                  ownsRows ? ri + width : rows, rows};
+        const size_t colCuts[4] = {0, ownsCols ? cj : cols,
+                                  ownsCols ? cj + width : cols, cols};
+        for (int r = 0; r < 4; r += 2) {
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; c += 2) {
+                updateRectangle(dist, path, rowPanel, colPanel, rows, cols, width, first,
+                                rowCuts[r], rowCuts[r + 1], colCuts[c], colCuts[c + 1]);
+            }
+        }
+        first += width;
+    }
+}
+
+// Only materialize the global matrix when the requested output needs it.
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                                      size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(n * n);
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(grid.comm, &processes);
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < processes; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const Partition rows = partition(n, grid.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const Partition cols = partition(n, grid.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows.size == 0 || cols.size == 0) continue;
+            unsigned int* destination = result.data() + rows.begin * n + cols.begin;
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(local.data() + i * cols.size, cols.size, destination + i * n);
                 }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype block;
+                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows.size), static_cast<int>(cols.size),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), MPI_UNSIGNED, &block);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+                MPI_Recv(destination, 1, block, rank, 1, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&block);
             }
         }
+    } else if (!local.empty()) {
+        MPI_Datatype row;
+        MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(grid.cols.size), MPI_UNSIGNED, &row);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&row);
+        MPI_Send(local.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.size), row, 0, 1, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&row);
     }
+    return result;
 }

+struct Partition {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+Partition partition(size_t n, int parts, int coordinate) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    return {coordinate * base + std::min(extra, static_cast<size_t>(coordinate)),
+            base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < extra)};
+}

+int owner(size_t k, size_t n, int parts) {
+    const size_t base = n / parts;
+    const size_t extra = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (base + 1) * extra;
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (base + 1)
+                                         : extra + (k - boundary) / base);
+}

+struct ProcessGrid {
+    MPI_Comm comm;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm;
+    MPI_Comm colComm;
+    int rank;
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    int coords[2];
+    Partition rows;
+    Partition cols;
+
+    explicit ProcessGrid(size_t n) {
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processes);
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dims);
+        const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 2, coords);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[0], coords[1], &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(comm, coords[1], coords[0], &colComm);
+        rows = partition(n, dims[0], coords[0]);
+        cols = partition(n, dims[1], coords[1]);

+    void free() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&comm);

+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per result. Exponentiate
+// its affine transformation to jump directly to any position in the ORIGINAL
+// random stream; changing the seed per rank would change the benchmark graph.
+struct RandomJump {
+    unsigned int multiplier = 1;
+    unsigned int increment = 0;
+
+    explicit RandomJump(size_t samples) {
+        unsigned int a = 1;
+        unsigned int c = 0;
+        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
+            a *= 1103515245u;
+            c = c * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        }
+        while (samples != 0) {
+            if (samples & 1) {
+                increment = increment * a + c;
+                multiplier *= a;
+            }
+            c *= a + 1;
+            a *= a;
+            samples >>= 1;
+        }
     }
+
+    unsigned int operator()(unsigned int seed) const {
+        return multiplier * seed + increment;
+    }
+};
+#endif
+
+void initializeMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path, size_t n,
+                        const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
+#if defined(__GLIBC__)
+    if (rows != 0 && cols != 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = RandomJump(grid.rows.begin * n + grid.cols.begin)(42);
+        const RandomJump nextRow(n - cols);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+                dist[i * cols + j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            seed = nextRow(seed);
+        }
+    }
+#else
+    // Other libc implementations need not use the same RNG recurrence.
+    // Stream one original row at a time without allocating a global matrix.
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        std::vector<unsigned int> row(n);
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                row[j] = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
+                    range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
+            }
+            for (int c = 0; c < grid.dims[1]; ++c) {
+                const Partition columns = partition(n, grid.dims[1], c);
+                if (columns.size == 0) continue;
+                int coordinates[2] = {owner(i, n, grid.dims[0]), c};
+                int target;
+                MPI_Cart_rank(grid.comm, coordinates, &target);
+                if (target == 0) {
+                    std::copy_n(row.data() + columns.begin, columns.size,
+                                dist.data() + (i - grid.rows.begin) * cols);
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(row.data() + columns.begin, static_cast<int>(columns.size),
+                             MPI_UNSIGNED, target, 0, grid.comm);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (cols != 0) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data() + i * cols, static_cast<int>(cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                     0, 0, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+    }
+#endif
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < cols; ++j) {
+            path[i * cols + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.cols.begin + j);
+            if (grid.rows.begin + i == grid.cols.begin + j) dist[i * cols + j] = 0;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Independent, contiguous updates permit SIMD without changing strict tie
+// handling or the original meaning of path[i,j] (last improving pivot).
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivot,
+                      unsigned int via, size_t count, unsigned int k) {
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = via + pivot[j];
+        const unsigned int previous = dist[j];
+        dist[j] = std::min(previous, candidate);
+        path[j] = candidate < previous ? k : path[j];
+    }
 }

+void updateRectangle(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& rowPanel,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& colPanel,
+                     size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t width, size_t firstPivot,
+                     size_t rowBegin, size_t rowEnd, size_t colBegin, size_t colEnd) {
+    // Keep distance/path tiles in cache while replaying a whole pivot batch.
+    constexpr size_t tileRows = 32;
+    constexpr size_t tileCols = 256;
+    for (size_t ib = rowBegin; ib < rowEnd; ib += tileRows) {
+        const size_t ie = std::min(ib + tileRows, rowEnd);

+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                const unsigned int* pivot = rowPanel.data() + t * cols + jb;
+                for (size_t i = ib; i < ie; ++i) {
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + i * cols + jb,
+                              path.data() + i * cols + jb, pivot,

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    const size_t rows = grid.rows.size;
+    const size_t cols = grid.cols.size;
+    // Bound MPI counts even when a panel would otherwise exceed INT_MAX.
+    const size_t maxEdge = std::max<size_t>(1, (n + grid.dims[1] - 1) / grid.dims[1]);
+    const size_t batch = std::min<size_t>(64, INT_MAX / maxEdge);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> diagonalRows(batch * batch);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> diagonalCols(batch * batch);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> rowPanel(batch * cols);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> colPanel(batch * rows);
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < n;) {
+        const int rowOwner = owner(first, n, grid.dims[0]);
+        const int colOwner = owner(first, n, grid.dims[1]);
+        const Partition pivotRows = partition(n, grid.dims[0], rowOwner);
+        const Partition pivotCols = partition(n, grid.dims[1], colOwner);
+        const size_t width = std::min({batch, pivotRows.begin + pivotRows.size - first,
+                                      pivotCols.begin + pivotCols.size - first});
+        const bool ownsRows = grid.coords[0] == rowOwner;
+        const bool ownsCols = grid.coords[1] == colOwner;
+        const size_t ri = ownsRows ? first - grid.rows.begin : 0;
+        const size_t cj = ownsCols ? first - grid.cols.begin : 0;
+
+        // 1. Solve the diagonal tile, retaining its row/column at EACH k.
+        // Using only its final values would change strict-improvement paths.
+        if (ownsRows && ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols + cj, width,
+                            diagonalRows.data() + t * width);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    diagonalCols[t * width + i] = dist[(ri + i) * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols + cj,
+                              path.data() + (ri + i) * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width,
+                              diagonalCols[t * width + i], width,
+                              static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Request requests[2];
+        int requestCount = 0;
+        if (ownsRows) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalCols.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            MPI_Ibcast(diagonalRows.data(), static_cast<int>(width * width), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                       rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        }
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 2. Solve the pivot row/column panels, saving the chronological
+        // pivot values needed by all the independent remaining rectangles.
+        if (ownsRows && cols != 0) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                std::copy_n(dist.data() + (ri + t) * cols, cols,
+                            rowPanel.data() + t * cols);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                    unsigned int* d = dist.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    unsigned int* p = path.data() + (ri + i) * cols;
+                    const unsigned int via = diagonalCols[t * width + i];
+                    if (ownsCols) {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cj,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                        const size_t after = cj + width;
+                        updateRow(d + after, p + after, rowPanel.data() + t * cols + after,
+                                  via, cols - after, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    } else {
+                        updateRow(d, p, rowPanel.data() + t * cols, via, cols,
+                                  static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (ownsCols) {
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < width; ++t) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    colPanel[t * rows + i] = dist[i * cols + cj + t];
+                }
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    if (ownsRows && i >= ri && i < ri + width) continue;
+                    updateRow(dist.data() + i * cols + cj, path.data() + i * cols + cj,
+                              diagonalRows.data() + t * width, colPanel[t * rows + i],
+                              width, static_cast<unsigned int>(first + t));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Ibcast(rowPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * cols), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   rowOwner, grid.colComm, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Ibcast(colPanel.data(), static_cast<int>(width * rows), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                   colOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        // 3. Replay exactly the original increasing-k updates locally.
+        const size_t rowCuts[4] = {0, ownsRows ? ri : rows,
+                                  ownsRows ? ri + width : rows, rows};
+        const size_t colCuts[4] = {0, ownsCols ? cj : cols,
+                                  ownsCols ? cj + width : cols, cols};
+        for (int r = 0; r < 4; r += 2) {
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; c += 2) {
+                updateRectangle(dist, path, rowPanel, colPanel, rows, cols, width, first,
+                                rowCuts[r], rowCuts[r + 1], colCuts[c], colCuts[c + 1]);
+            }
+        }
+        first += width;
+    }
+}

+// Only materialize the global matrix when the requested output needs it.
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                                      size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (grid.rank == 0) {
+        result.resize(n * n);
+        int processes;
+        MPI_Comm_size(grid.comm, &processes);
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < processes; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid.comm, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const Partition rows = partition(n, grid.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const Partition cols = partition(n, grid.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+            if (rows.size == 0 || cols.size == 0) continue;
+            unsigned int* destination = result.data() + rows.begin * n + cols.begin;
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(local.data() + i * cols.size, cols.size, destination + i * n);
                 }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype block;
+                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows.size), static_cast<int>(cols.size),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), MPI_UNSIGNED, &block);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&block);
+                MPI_Recv(destination, 1, block, rank, 1, grid.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&block);
             }
         }
+    } else if (!local.empty()) {
+        MPI_Datatype row;
+        MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(grid.cols.size), MPI_UNSIGNED, &row);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&row);
+        MPI_Send(local.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.size), row, 0, 1, grid.comm);
+        MPI_Type_free(&row);
     }
+    return result;
 }
